2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAxolotl is a free and open-source tool designed to streamline post-training and fine-tuning for the latest large language models (LLMs).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFeatures:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMultiple Model Support\u003c/strong\u003e: Train various models like GPT-OSS, LLaMA, Mistral, Mixtral, Pythia, and many more models available on the Hugging Face Hub.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMultimodal Training\u003c/strong\u003e: Fine-tune vision-language models (VLMs) including LLaMA-Vision, Qwen2-VL, Pixtral, LLaVA, SmolVLM2, GLM-4.6V, InternVL 3.5, Gemma 3n, PaddleOCR-VL, and audio models like Voxtral with image, video, and audio support.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTraining Methods\u003c/strong\u003e: Full fine-tuning, LoRA, QLoRA, GPTQ, QAT (int8/int4/FP8/NVFP4/MXFP4), FP8 mixed-precision training, NVFP4/MXFP4 MoE LoRA, Preference Tuning (DPO, IPO, KTO, ORPO), RL (GRPO, GDPO), and Reward Modelling (RM) / Process Reward Modelling (PRM).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEasy Configuration\u003c/strong\u003e: Re-use a single YAML configuration file across the full fine-tuning pipeline: dataset preprocessing, training, evaluation, quantization, and inference.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePerformance Optimizations\u003c/strong\u003e: \u003ca <li>2025/07:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>ND Parallelism support has been added into Axolotl. Compose Context Parallelism (CP), Tensor Parallelism (TP), and Fully Sharded Data Parallelism (FSDP) within a single node and across multiple nodes. Check out the <a href="https://huggingface.co/blog/accelerate-nd-parallel" rel="nofollow">blog post</a> for more info.</li>
<li>Axolotl adds more models: <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/models/gpt-oss.html" rel="nofollow">GPT-OSS</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/models/gemma3n.html" rel="nofollow">Gemma 3n</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/models/LiquidAI.html" rel="nofollow">Liquid Foundation Model 2 (LFM2)</a>, and <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/models/arcee.html" rel="nofollow">Arcee Foundation Models (AFM)</a>.</li>
<li>FP8 finetuning with fp8 gather op is now possible in Axolotl via <code>torchao</code>. Get started <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/mixed_precision.html#sec-fp8" rel="nofollow">here</a>!</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/models/voxtral.html" rel="nofollow">Voxtral</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/models/magistral.html" rel="nofollow">Magistral 1.1</a>, and <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/models/devstral.html" rel="nofollow">Devstral</a> with mistral-common tokenizer support has been integrated in Axolotl!</li>
<li>TiledMLP support for single-GPU to multi-GPU training with DDP, DeepSpeed and FSDP support has been added to support Arctic Long Sequence Training. (ALST). See <a href="https://github.com/axolotl-ai-cloud/axolotl/tree/main/examples/alst">examples</a> for using ALST with Axolotl!</li>
</ul>

<li>2025/05: Quantization Aware Training (QAT) support has been added to Axolotl. Explore the <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/qat.html" rel="nofollow">docs</a> to learn more!</li>
<li>2025/04: Llama 4 support has been added in Axolotl. See <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/models/llama-4.html" rel="nofollow">docs</a> to start training your own Llama 4 models with Axolotl's linearized version!</li>
<li>2025/03: Axolotl has implemented Sequence Parallelism (SP) support. Read the <a href="https://huggingface.co/blog/axolotl-ai-co/long-context-with-sequence-parallelism-in-axolotl" rel="nofollow">blog</a> and <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/sequence_parallelism.html" rel="nofollow">docs</a> to learn how to scale your context length when fine-tuning.</li>
<li>2025/03: (Beta) Fine-tuning Multimodal models is now supported in Axolotl. Check out the <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/multimodal.html" rel="nofollow">docs</a> to fine-tune your own!</li>
<li>2025/02: Axolotl has added LoRA optimizations to reduce memory usage and improve training speed for LoRA and QLoRA in single GPU and multi-GPU training (DDP and DeepSpeed). Jump into the <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/lora_optims.html" rel="nofollow">docs</a> to give it a try.</li>
<li>2025/02: Axolotl has added GRPO support. Dive into our <a href="https://huggingface.co/blog/axolotl-ai-co/training-llms-w-interpreter-feedback-wasm" rel="nofollow">blog</a> and <a href="https://github.com/axolotl-ai-cloud/grpo_code">GRPO example</a> and have some fun!</li>
<li>2025/01: Axolotl has added Reward Modelling / Process Reward Modelling fine-tuning support. See <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/reward_modelling.html" rel="nofollow">docs</a>.</li>
</ul>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">✨ Overview</h2><a id="user-content--overview" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ✨ Overview" href="#-overview"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Axolotl is a free and open-source tool designed to streamline post-training and fine-tuning for the latest large language models (LLMs).</p>
<p dir="auto">Features:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Multiple Model Support</strong>: Train various models like GPT-OSS, LLaMA, Mistral, Mixtral, Pythia, and many more models available on the Hugging Face Hub.</li>
<li><strong>Multimodal Training</strong>: Fine-tune vision-language models (VLMs) including LLaMA-Vision, Qwen2-VL, Pixtral, LLaVA, SmolVLM2, GLM-4.6V, InternVL 3.5, Gemma 3n, PaddleOCR-VL, and audio models like Voxtral with image, video, and audio support.</li>
<li><strong>Training Methods</strong>: Full fine-tuning, LoRA, QLoRA, GPTQ, QAT (int8/int4/FP8/NVFP4/MXFP4), FP8 mixed-precision training, NVFP4/MXFP4 MoE LoRA, Preference Tuning (DPO, IPO, KTO, ORPO), RL (GRPO, GDPO), and Reward Modelling (RM) / Process Reward Modelling (PRM).</li>
<li><strong>Easy Configuration</strong>: Re-use a single YAML configuration file across the full fine-tuning pipeline: dataset preprocessing, training, evaluation, quantization, and inference.</li>
<li><strong>Performance Optimizations</strong>: <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/multipack.html" rel="nofollow">Multipacking</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/attention.html#flash-attention" rel="nofollow">Flash Attention 2/3/4</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/attention.html#xformers" rel="nofollow">Xformers</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/attention.html#flex-attention" rel="nofollow">Flex Attention</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/attention.html#sageattention" rel="nofollow">SageAttention</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/custom_integrations.html#liger-kernels" rel="nofollow">Liger Kernel</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/custom_integrations.html#cut-cross-entropy" rel="nofollow">Cut Cross Entropy</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/custom_integrations.html#kernels-integration" rel="nofollow">ScatterMoE</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/sequence_parallelism.html" rel="nofollow">Sequence Parallelism (SP)</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/lora_optims.html" rel="nofollow">LoRA optimizations</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/multi-gpu.html" rel="nofollow">Multi-GPU training (FSDP1, FSDP2, DeepSpeed)</a>, <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/multi-node.html" rel="nofollow">Multi-node training (Torchrun, Ray)</a>, and many more!</li>
<li><strong>Flexible Dataset Handling</strong>: Load from local, HuggingFace, and cloud (S3, Azure, GCP, OCI) datasets.</li>
<li><strong>Cloud Ready</strong>: We ship <a href="https://hub.docker.com/u/axolotlai" rel="nofollow">Docker images</a> and also <a href="https://pypi.org/project/axolotl/" rel="nofollow">PyPI packages</a> for use on cloud platforms and local hardware.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚀 Quick Start - LLM Fine-tuning in Minutes</h2><a id="user-content--quick-start---llm-fine-tuning-in-minutes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚀 Quick Start - LLM Fine-tuning in Minutes" href="#-quick-start---llm-fine-tuning-in-minutes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Requirements</strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>NVIDIA GPU (Ampere or newer for <code>bf16</code> and Flash Attention) or AMD GPU</li>
<li>Python &gt;=3.11 (3.12 recommended)</li>
<li>PyTorch ≥2.11.0</li>
</ul>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h3><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# install uv if you don't already have it installed (restart shell after)
curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh

# change depending on system
export UV_TORCH_BACKEND=cu130

# create a new virtual environment
uv venv --python 3.12
source .venv/bin/activate

uv pip install torch==2.12.0 torchvision
uv pip install --no-build-isolation axolotl[deepspeed]

# Download example axolotl configs, deepspeed configs
axolotl fetch examples
axolotl fetch deepspeed_configs  # OPTIONAL"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> install uv if you don't already have it installed (restart shell after)</span>
curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> sh

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> change depending on system</span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> UV_TORCH_BACKEND=cu130

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> create a new virtual environment</span>
uv venv --python 3.12
<span class="pl-c1">source</span> .venv/bin/activate

uv pip install torch==2.12.0 torchvision
uv pip install --no-build-isolation axolotl[deepspeed]

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Download example axolotl configs, deepspeed configs</span>
axolotl fetch examples
axolotl fetch deepspeed_configs  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> OPTIONAL</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Using Docker</h4><a id="user-content-using-docker" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Using Docker" href="#using-docker"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Installing with Docker can be less error prone than installing in your own environment.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run --gpus '&quot;all&quot;' --ipc=host --rm -it axolotlai/axolotl:main-latest"><pre>docker run --gpus <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>"all"<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> --ipc=host --rm -it axolotlai/axolotl:main-latest</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Other installation approaches are described <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/installation.html" rel="nofollow">here</a>.</p>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Fetch axolotl examples
axolotl fetch examples

# Or, specify a custom path
axolotl fetch examples --dest path/to/folder

# Train a model using LoRA
axolotl train examples/llama-3/lora-1b.yml"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Fetch axolotl examples</span>
axolotl fetch examples

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or, specify a custom path</span>
axolotl fetch examples --dest path/to/folder

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Train a model using LoRA</span>
axolotl train examples/llama-3/lora-1b.yml</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">That's it! Check out our <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/getting-started.html" rel="nofollow">Getting Started Guide</a> for a more detailed walkthrough.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">AI Agent Support</h2><a id="user-content-ai-agent-support" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: AI Agent Support" href="#ai-agent-support"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Axolotl ships with built-in documentation optimized for AI coding agents (Claude Code, Cursor, Copilot, etc.). These docs are bundled with the pip package, no repo clone needed.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Show overview and available training methods
axolotl agent-docs

# Topic-specific references
axolotl agent-docs sft                 # supervised fine-tuning
axolotl agent-docs grpo                # GRPO online RL
axolotl agent-docs preference_tuning   # DPO, KTO, ORPO, SimPO
axolotl agent-docs reward_modelling    # outcome and process reward models
axolotl agent-docs pretraining         # continual pretraining
axolotl agent-docs --list              # list all topics

# Dump config schema for programmatic use
axolotl config-schema
axolotl config-schema --field adapter"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Show overview and available training methods</span>
axolotl agent-docs

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Topic-specific references</span>
axolotl agent-docs sft                 <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> supervised fine-tuning</span>
axolotl agent-docs grpo                <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> GRPO online RL</span>
axolotl agent-docs preference_tuning   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> DPO, KTO, ORPO, SimPO</span>
axolotl agent-docs reward_modelling    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> outcome and process reward models</span>
axolotl agent-docs pretraining         <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> continual pretraining</span>
axolotl agent-docs --list              <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> list all topics</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Dump config schema for programmatic use</span>
axolotl config-schema
axolotl config-schema --field adapter</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If you're working with the source repo, agent docs are also available at <code>docs/agents/</code> and the project overview is in <code>AGENTS.md</code>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📈 Telemetry</h2><a id="user-content--telemetry" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📈 Telemetry" href="#-telemetry"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Axolotl has opt-out telemetry that helps us understand how the project is being used
and prioritize improvements. We collect basic system information, model types, and
error rates, never personal data or file paths. Telemetry is enabled by default. To
disable it, set AXOLOTL_DO_NOT_TRACK=1. For more details, see our <a href="https://docs.axolotl.ai/docs/telemetry.html" rel="nofollow">telemetry documentation</a>.</p>
